written in CI vectors:
$$+1 \cdot{} \left|0220\right| \cdot{} \left|aaaa\right| +1 \cdot{} \left|aaaa\right| \cdot{} \left|0220\right|$$or, expressed in terms of symmetry:
monomer determinants:
\begin{multline*}+1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{1u}^{r}\right)\left(b_{2g}^{r}\right)\left(b_{3g}^{r}\right)\left(a_{u}^{r}\right)\right| +1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{1u}^{l}\right)\left(b_{2g}^{l}\right)\left(b_{3g}^{l}\right)\left(a_{u}^{l}\right)\right|\end{multline*}substitution by dimer orbitals:
\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}
+1 \cdot{} \left| \left(-a_{g}+b_{1u}\right)\left(-b_{3u}+b_{2g}\right)\left(-b_{2u}+b_{3g}\right)\left(-b_{1g}+a_{u}\right)\right| +1 \cdot{} \left| \left(+a_{g}+b_{1u}\right)\left(+b_{3u}+b_{2g}\right)\left(+b_{2u}+b_{3g}\right)\left(+b_{1g}+a_{u}\right)\right|
\end{array}\end{multline*}multiplied out:
\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}
+ \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1g} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{2u}a_{u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{1g}b_{3g} }_{b_{1u}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{2u}b_{1g}b_{2g} }_{b_{1u}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{2u}b_{2g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] + \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{1g}b_{2g}b_{3g} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{2g}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1g}b_{1u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1u}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{1g}b_{1u}b_{3g} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{1u}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| \\[0.5cm] + \left| \underbrace{ b_{2u}b_{1g}b_{1u}b_{2g} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ b_{2u}b_{1u}b_{2g}a_{u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ b_{1g}b_{1u}b_{2g}b_{3g} }_{b_{1u}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{1u}b_{2g}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1g} }_{a_{g}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{2u}a_{u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| \\[0.5cm] + \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{1g}b_{3g} }_{b_{1u}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{2u}b_{1g}b_{2g} }_{b_{1u}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{2u}b_{2g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{1g}b_{2g}b_{3g} }_{a_{g}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{2g}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| \\[0.5cm] + \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1g}b_{1u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1u}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{1g}b_{1u}b_{3g} }_{a_{g}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{1u}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{2u}b_{1g}b_{1u}b_{2g} }_{a_{g}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{2u}b_{1u}b_{2g}a_{u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| \\[0.5cm] + \left| \underbrace{ b_{1g}b_{1u}b_{2g}b_{3g} }_{b_{1u}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{1u}b_{2g}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right|
\end{array}\end{multline*}
summarized:
\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}
+2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1g} }_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{2u}b_{2g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{1g}b_{2g}b_{3g} }_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1u}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{1g}b_{1u}b_{3g} }_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] +2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ b_{2u}b_{1g}b_{1u}b_{2g} }_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ b_{1u}b_{2g}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right|
\end{array}\end{multline*}

written in CI vectors:
$$+1 \cdot{} \left|0220\right| \cdot{} \left|aaaa\right| -1 \cdot{} \left|aaaa\right| \cdot{} \left|0220\right|$$or, expressed in terms of symmetry:
monomer determinants:
\begin{multline*}+1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{1u}^{r}\right)\left(b_{2g}^{r}\right)\left(b_{3g}^{r}\right)\left(a_{u}^{r}\right)\right| -1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{1u}^{l}\right)\left(b_{2g}^{l}\right)\left(b_{3g}^{l}\right)\left(a_{u}^{l}\right)\right|\end{multline*}substitution by dimer orbitals:
\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}
+1 \cdot{} \left| \left(-a_{g}+b_{1u}\right)\left(-b_{3u}+b_{2g}\right)\left(-b_{2u}+b_{3g}\right)\left(-b_{1g}+a_{u}\right)\right| -1 \cdot{} \left| \left(+a_{g}+b_{1u}\right)\left(+b_{3u}+b_{2g}\right)\left(+b_{2u}+b_{3g}\right)\left(+b_{1g}+a_{u}\right)\right|
\end{array}\end{multline*}multiplied out:
\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}
+ \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1g} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{2u}a_{u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{1g}b_{3g} }_{b_{1u}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{2u}b_{1g}b_{2g} }_{b_{1u}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{2u}b_{2g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] + \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{1g}b_{2g}b_{3g} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{2g}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1g}b_{1u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1u}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{1g}b_{1u}b_{3g} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{1u}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| \\[0.5cm] + \left| \underbrace{ b_{2u}b_{1g}b_{1u}b_{2g} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ b_{2u}b_{1u}b_{2g}a_{u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ b_{1g}b_{1u}b_{2g}b_{3g} }_{b_{1u}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{1u}b_{2g}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1g} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{2u}a_{u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| \\[0.5cm] - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{1g}b_{3g} }_{b_{1u}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{2u}b_{1g}b_{2g} }_{b_{1u}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{2u}b_{2g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{1g}b_{2g}b_{3g} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{2g}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| \\[0.5cm] - \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1g}b_{1u} }_{b_{1u}}\right| - monomer determinants:
\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}
+1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{1u}^{l}\right)\left(b_{3g}^{l}\right)\left(b_{1u}^{r}\right)\left(b_{3g}^{r}\right)\right| -1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{1u}^{l}\right)\left(b_{3g}^{l}\right)\left(b_{2g}^{r}\right)\left(a_{u}^{r}\right)\right| -1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{2g}^{l}\right)\left(a_{u}^{l}\right)\left(b_{1u}^{r}\right)\left(b_{3g}^{r}\right)\right| +1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{2g}^{l}\right)\left(a_{u}^{l}\right)\left(b_{2g}^{r}\right)\left(a_{u}^{r}\right)\right|

\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}

\end{array}\end{multline*}multiplied out:

+ \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}a_{g}b_{2u}b_{2u} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}a_{g}b_{3g}b_{3g} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ b_{2u}b_{2u}b_{1u}b_{1u} }_{a_{g}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{1u}b_{1u}b_{3g}b_{3g} }_{a_{g}}\right| -2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1g} }_{a_{g}}\right| -2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{2u}b_{2g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] +2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{1g}b_{2g}b_{3g} }_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1u}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ b_{2u}b_{1g}b_{1u}b_{2g} }_{a_{g}}\right| -2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{1g}b_{1u}b_{3g} }_{a_{g}}\right| -2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ b_{1u}b_{2g}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] + \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{3u}b_{1g}b_{1g} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{3u}a_{u}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ b_{1g}b_{1g}b_{2g}b_{2g} }_{a_{g}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{2g}b_{2g}a_{u}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right|

written in CI vectors:
$$\begin{array}{c}

monomer determinants:
\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}

\left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{2u}a_{u}a_{u} }_{b_{1g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] - \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{1g}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{b_{1g}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{3g}a_{u}a_{u} }_{a_{u}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{2u}b_{1g}b_{1g}b_{2g} }_{a_{u}}\right| - written in CI vectors:
$$\begin{array}{c}
+1 \cdot{} \left|a2a0\right| \cdot{} \left|aa20\right| +1 \cdot{} \left|aa20\right| \cdot{} \left|a2a0\right| +1 \cdot{} \left|a2a0\right| \cdot{} \left|02aa\right| +1 \cdot{} \left|02aa\right| \cdot{} \left|a2a0\right| \\[0.5cm] -1 \cdot{} \left|0a2a\right| \cdot{} \left|aa20\right| -1 \cdot{} \left|aa20\right| \cdot{} \left|0a2a\right| -1 \cdot{} \left|0a2a\right| \cdot{} \left|02aa\right| -1 \cdot{} \left|02aa\right| \cdot{} \left|0a2a\right|
\end{array}$$or, expressed in terms of symmetry:
monomer determinants:
\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}
+1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{1u}^{l}\right)\left(b_{3g}^{l}\right)\left(b_{1u}^{r}\right)\left(b_{2g}^{r}\right)\right| +1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{1u}^{l}\right)\left(b_{2g}^{l}\right)\left(b_{1u}^{r}\right)\left(b_{3g}^{r}\right)\right| +1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{1u}^{l}\right)\left(b_{3g}^{l}\right)\left(b_{3g}^{r}\right)\left(a_{u}^{r}\right)\right| +1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{3g}^{l}\right)\left(a_{u}^{l}\right)\left(b_{1u}^{r}\right)\left(b_{3g}^{r}\right)\right| \\[0.5cm] -1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{2g}^{l}\right)\left(a_{u}^{l}\right)\left(b_{1u}^{r}\right)\left(b_{2g}^{r}\right)\right| -1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{1u}^{l}\right)\left(b_{2g}^{l}\right)\left(b_{2g}^{r}\right)\left(a_{u}^{r}\right)\right| -1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{2g}^{l}\right)\left(a_{u}^{l}\right)\left(b_{3g}^{r}\right)\left(a_{u}^{r}\right)\right| -1 \cdot{} \left| \left(b_{3g}^{l}\right)\left(a_{u}^{l}\right)\left(b_{2g}^{r}\right)\left(a_{u}^{r}\right)\right|

\left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{3g}a_{u}a_{u} }_{a_{u}}\right| \\[0.5cm] - \left| \underbrace{ b_{1g}b_{2g}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{a_{u}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{2g}b_{3g}a_{u}a_{u} }_{b_{1g}}\right|

$$\begin{array}{c}
+1 \cdot{} \left|a2a0\right| \cdot{} \left|aa20\right| -1 \cdot{} \left|aa20\right| \cdot{} \left|a2a0\right| +1 \cdot{} \left|a2a0\right| \cdot{} \left|02aa\right| -1 \cdot{} \left|02aa\right| \cdot{} \left|a2a0\right| \\[0.5cm] +1 \cdot{} \left|0a2a\right| \cdot{} \left|aa20\right| -1 \cdot{} \left|aa20\right| \cdot{} \left|0a2a\right| +1 \cdot{} \left|0a2a\right| \cdot{} \left|02aa\right| -1 \cdot{} \left|02aa\right| \cdot{} \left|0a2a\right|
\end{array}$$or, expressed in terms of symmetry:
monomer determinants:
\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}

monomer determinants:
\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}

\end{array}\end{multline*}substitution by dimer orbitals:
\begin{multline*}\begin{array}{c}

\end{array}\end{multline*}multiplied out:

+ \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}a_{g}b_{3u}b_{3u} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}a_{g}b_{2g}b_{2g} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{3u}b_{1u}b_{1u} }_{a_{g}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{1u}b_{1u}b_{2g}b_{2g} }_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1g} }_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{3u}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] -2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{2u}b_{2g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| -2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ a_{g}b_{1g}b_{2g}b_{3g} }_{a_{g}}\right| -2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{2u}b_{1u}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| -2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ b_{3u}b_{1g}b_{1u}b_{3g} }_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ b_{2u}b_{1g}b_{1u}b_{2g} }_{a_{g}}\right| +2 \cdot{}  \left| \underbrace{ b_{1u}b_{2g}b_{3g}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| \\[0.5cm] + \left| \underbrace{ b_{2u}b_{2u}b_{1g}b_{1g} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ b_{2u}b_{2u}a_{u}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right| - \left| \underbrace{ b_{1g}b_{1g}b_{3g}b_{3g} }_{a_{g}}\right| + \left| \underbrace{ b_{3g}b_{3g}a_{u}a_{u} }_{a_{g}}\right|
\end{array}\end{multline*}
